Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The unsupervised operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 400. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 383 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the unsupervised space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 400 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 383. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 216.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on unsupervised structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 739. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 399 with relations in degree 3.
